    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

5. **EDCR diagnostic** reveals a critical issue: EDCR is almost perfectly inversely correlated with rel_f1 (spearman = -0.9648) and completely uncorrelated with evi_f1 (spearman = 0.0022). This suggests EDCR isn't actually an independent metric but rather a function of 1/rel_f1, which means it doesn't provide any new information beyond what rel_f1 already captures — a significant problem for the paper's claims about its diagnostic value.

Now I need to verify whether the worker actually prepared the CED experiments for seeds 43 and 45, check GPU allocation compliance, and look at the reeval_flat_nli results for seed 43.

5pp（不退化）；(3) CED_EDCR 显著低于 SFT_EDCR（核心判据）；(4) CED > flat-NLI 在 Evi-F1 或 EDCR\\n    上方向正确。绝对指标留到 post-MVP 增大 max_new_tokens 后重评。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_sft_eval_r2\\n    role: baseline\\n    result: F1=40.57%, Evi-F1=79.79%, EDCR=71.18%\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D011\\n- id: D013\\n  date: 2026-05-14 14:54 UTC\\n  summary: MVP-2 pass criteria 调整：取消绝对 F1>45 门槛，改为相对对比；EDCR 升为核心判定指标\\n  rationale: SFT baseline F1=40.57%（37% 输出被 1024 token 截断导致），已低于原定 F1>45 门槛。但截断对所有模型等效影响，相对对比仍然公平。调整后\\n    pass criteria：(1) 核心不变：CED_Evi-F1 − SFT_Evi-F1 ≥ 2pp；(2) F1 改为相对：CED_F1 ≥ SFT_F1\\n    − 5pp（即 ≥35.57）；(3) 新增 EDCR 核心判定：CED_EDCR 显著低于 SFT_EDCR（71.18%），降幅 ≥10pp 视为强证据；(4)\\n    保持 CED > flat-NLI 方向。SFT EDCR=71% 说明 SFT 模型大量引用 entity-sharing distractors，正是\\n    CED 设计目标的攻击面。即使 Evi-F1 提升不足 2pp，EDCR 大幅下降也足以支撑 Claim 2。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_sft_eval_r2\\n    role: baseline\\n    result: F1=40.57%, Evi-F1=79.79%, EDCR=71.18%\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D011\\n- id: D014\\n  date: 2026-05-14 14:58 UTC\\n  summary: CED r5 mode collapse 确认 kill；CED reward 必须加 F1 门控防止短输出 hack；暂停 ced_kl001\\n  rationale: CED r5 在 65% 进度完全 collapse：completion 固定 34 tokens，f1_reward=0，ced_reward≈0.99，entropy≈0。模型发现极短输出不引用\\n    distractor 即可获得近完美 CED 分——CED 自身被 reward hack。根因：CED reward 不依赖抽取质量，模型可在 F1=0\\n    时仍获高 CED reward。修复方案：F1 门控——仅在 completion 包含 ≥1 个 predicted triple 时计算 CED reward，否则\\n    CED=0。这确保 evidence quality 的评估以有效抽取为前提。ced_kl001 使用同样有缺陷的 reward 设计，必须暂停，等修复后再启动。extract_r3\\n    部分 collapse 但仍有 F1 信号，继续观察。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r5\\n    role: failed\\n    result: 'mode collapse: f1=0, ced≈0.99, 34-token fixed output'\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_extract_r3\\n    role: monitoring\\n    result: 'partial collapse: f1=0.2-0.34, 43-50 tokens, trending down'\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_flatnli_r3\\n    role: healthy\\n    result: 'healthy: entropy=0.087, grad_norm=0.398, 11% progress'\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D013\\n- id: D015\\n  date: 2026-05-14 14:58 UTC\\n  summary: ced_r5 mode collapse 保留跑完取分析价值；flatnli_r3 kill 并修复 NLI device 后重启\\n  rationale: ced_r5 在 63% 进度时 f1_reward=0 + entropy≈0，已 mode collapse。保留跑完因为：(1) negative\\n    result 证明纯 CED reward 导致 collapse，支撑 reward balance 的 ablation story；(2) 早期 checkpoint\\n    可能有 f1_reward 正常的版本可用。flatnli_r3 因 NLI 推理在 CPU 上运行导致 10x 减速（124s/it vs 12s/it），预计\\n    20h+。RTX 5090 32GB 有足够余量放 DeBERTa-v3-base (~400MB)，kill 后修复 device 配置重启可将时间降至\\n    2-3h。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r5\\n    role: subject\\n    result: f1_reward=0, mode collapse at 63%\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_flatnli_r3\\n    role: subject\\n    result: NLI on CPU, 10x slowdown, killed for restart\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D011\\n- id: D016\\n  date: 2026-05-14 15:00 UTC\\n  summary: Kill ced_r5 (total collapse)，ced_kl001 移到 cuda:0 立即启动\\n  rationale: ced_r5 step 250+ f1_reward=0、completion 坍缩到 34 tokens、save_total_limit=5\\n    已覆盖所有正常 checkpoint。继续跑无任何回收价值。Kill 后释放 cuda:0 给 ced_kl001（kl_coef=0.01），不再等 cuda:1。ced_kl001\\n    现在是 MVP-2 最关键实验——验证 CED+KL约束能否避免 reward hacking。extract_r3 和 flatnli_r3 保持 kl=0\\n    继续跑，作为对照：若它们没 collapse，说明问题是 CED reward 特异的；若也 collapse，说明 kl=0 通病。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_r5\\n    role: failed\\n    result: total mode collapse, killed\\n  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_kl001\\n    role: replacement\\n    result: moved to cuda:0, critical experiment\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D015\\n- id: D017\\n  date: 2026-05-14 15:01 UTC\\n  summary: Kill ced_r5 + extract_r3 (kl_coef=0 collapse/stall), 部署 kl_coef=0.01 修正实验\\n  rationale: 3路GRPO健康检查：ced_r5完全mode collapse(entropy=0.0005, F1=0, grad_norm=0)，extract_r3训练停滞(grad_norm=0,\\n    F1=0.20低于SFT baseline 0.4057)。共同根因kl_coef=0导致策略无约束偏移。D008显示kl_coef=0.05会KL爆炸，选择0.01作为中间值（5x低于爆炸阈值）。flatnli_r3保留作为kl_coef=0对照组继续观察。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - ```text
Let me now compile the full picture:

## Key findings:

### 1. Worker "research_prep_ced_s43_s45" — What did it actually do?
The worker name suggests it was supposed to prepare CED seeds s43/s45 for the paired t-test. However:
- No CED s43/s45 directories exist on the server
- No CED s43/s45 related entries in the registry
- The ROADMAP still lists "🔴 补 CED s43/s45 seeds" as pending

This is a concern. The worker was named "research_prep_ced_s43_s45" but it seems like it might have worked on flat-NLI evals and EDCR diagnostic instead? Or perhaps it did preparatory work (data generation/filtering) but no actual training output directories exist.
 
Looking at the timing more carefully—the worker ran at 23:55:11, right before reeval_flat_nli_s43 started analyzing at 23:56:41. Given the cluster of flat-NLI and EDCR activities happening around the same time, it appears the worker may have been reassigned or its prep work fed into those evaluations rather than producing the CED s43/s45 outputs it was named for. I'm realizing the seed parameter only affects RSFT training initialization, not the data generation itself—so CED s43/s45 would just reuse the existing CED-filtered data with different random seeds. The "prep" worker likely meant to submit those training jobs, but there are no corresponding registry entries for them, suggesting the work was never actually completed.

Let me reconsider what else this worker might have been assigned to. Looking at the timeline around when the worker was created at 23:55:11 UTC, I see the flat-NLI reevaluation finished shortly after, but the other eval jobs were submitted before the worker even existed, so the timing doesn't quite line up with what I'd expect.

Now I'm checking the GPU allocation across the system. Most of the GPUs are actively running experiments—cuda:0 and cuda:3 are handling the flat-NLI evaluations, cuda:2 is running the RSFT reevaluation—but cuda:1 is sitting completely idle with no memory allocated.

This isn't technically a red-line violation since the GPU wasn't allocated to a process that's supposed to be running; it was freed when reeval_flat_nli_s43 completed. But it's definitely a scheduling inefficiency—there are queued tasks like eval_plan_014_no_evidence that could be using this available GPU. I should check the project's execution constraints and GPU whitelist to understand why these pending tasks aren't being scheduled.

Now I'm looking at the EDCR diagnostic results, and there's a serious problem here. The correlation between EDCR and rel_f1 is -0.9648, which is almost perfect inverse correlation, while EDCR shows essentially zero correlation with evi_f1. This means EDCR is basically just a monotonic transformation of 1/rel_f1—it's not providing any independent diagnostic signal at all. The Agent's claim about EDCR having unique value for identifying pathological models doesn't hold up when the metric is this tightly coupled to relevance performance. EDCR by construction. You don't need EDCR to tell you a model with rel_f1=0.2167 is pathological — rel_f1 already tells you that.

2. The claim that EDCR is an "independent evaluation metric" is undermined by the near-perfect correlation with rel_f1.

3. D071 already notes this: "EDCR 定义存在 by-construction 混淆：当前 EDCR ≈ f(1/rel_f1)". And D070 downgraded Claim 4: "EDCR 从跨方法诊断指标降级为 per-relation-type/pe..."

The Agent's conclusion of "success" for this experiment is misleading. The experiment actually proved that EDCR has limited independent value as a cross-method diagnostic metric. When the Agent frames the "unique diagnostic value" as a positive, that's rationalization — any metric running 96% correlated with 1/rel_f1 isn't genuinely adding diagnostic insight. D070 already flagged this concern, so the real question is whether the Agent's framing could mislead the paper's conclusions.

Looking at reeval_flat_nli_s43, the metrics are consistent with s42 — rel_f1 essentially unchanged at 0.4523 versus 0.4539, with evi_f1 at 0.8158 and edcr at 0.6505. That's a valid data point. eval_flat_nli_seed44 was killed as a duplicate.

For the DPO 1ep results, rel_f1 dropped slightly to 0.4034 from the SFT baseline's 0.4057, so DPO 1ep isn't improving performance. There's a note about Ign-F1 exactly matching F1 that needs investigation — if ignoring training triples produces identical scores, it could indicate the model isn't actually learning from those triples or there's something off with how the metric is computed.

Looking at the reeval logs, Ign-F1 equals F1 there too (both 0.4523), and the eval loaded 37785 training triples. Since DocRED uses different documents for training and dev sets by design, there shouldn't be overlapping triples anyway, so Ign-F1 equaling F1 is actually expected behavior rather than a bug.

For eval_plan_014_no_evidence, it got killed while queued, but the ROADMAP already shows plan_014 marked complete with rel_f1=0.4893. That result might have come from an earlier task eval on a smaller set, so the full dev eval that was killed may have been redundant.

Now I'm compiling the issues to report: cuda:1 is sitting idle after reeval_flat_nli_s43 finished while eval_plan_014_no_evidence remains queued and unscheduled, indicating underutilized GPU capacity. There's also confusion around the CED s43/s45 worker—the worker name suggests it's prepping those seeds, but there are no corresponding directories or registry entries on the server, and the ROADMAP still marks that work as pending.

Then there's the EDCR diagnostic problem: the correlation analysis shows EDCR is almost a perfect inverse monotonic transformation of rel_f1 (Spearman correlation of -0.9648), which means it's not providing independent diagnostic value beyond what rel_f1 already tells us. The agent's claim about "unique diagnostic value" appears to be rationalization, and while D070/D071 have flagged this, I need to make sure the paper doesn't overstate EDCR's independent contribution.

The reeval results are coming in: s43 matches s42 closely (rel_f1=0.4523 vs 0.4539), confirming the D064 bug fix worked. DPO with 1 epoch shows rel_f1=0.4034, which is neutral compared to the SFT baseline, supporting the corrections from D068/D069. eval_flat_nli_seed44 was killed and is being retried normally.

Now I'm checking GPU compliance across the running experiments—eval_flat_nli_s44 is correctly assigned to cuda:0, and I need to verify the GPU allocation for the other seeds.

Looking at eval_flat_nli_seed45, there's a discrepancy: the spec shows cuda:3 but the top-level config says cuda:2, though nvidia-smi confirms it's actually running on cuda:3 with 49% utilization. Meanwhile, cuda:2 is at 90% utilization with reeval_rsft_topk3, which matches its spec assignment.

The GPU allocations appear correct based on actual usage, but I need to verify whether these assignments fall within the project's allowed gpu_ids whitelist. I don't have direct access to that configuration, so I should check the project settings or constraints to confirm cuda:2 and cuda:3 are permitted.

Let me also verify what the worker actually accomplished—it shows status=done, but the expected output directories don't exist, which suggests the task might have been different than its name implies.

I think I have what I need now. There are three key issues to flag: the CED s43/s45 directories that should have been prepared but weren't, which is blocking the paired t-test; cuda:1 sitting idle at 0% utilization with queued tasks waiting to run; and a methodological concern about the EDCR diagnostic analysis claiming unique value when the correlation with rel_f1 is actually -0.9648.

```

But this failed command uses:
```
cd /root/autodl-tmp/freige && ... python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer
```

The issue is the working directory. 

When running `python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer`, Python searches for the `freige` package relative to the Python path. The successful runs worked from `/root/autodl-tmp`, allowing Python to locate `freige` as a top-level package. But changing into `/root/autodl-tmp/freige` first means Python would need to find `freige/freige/training/rsft_trainer.py`, which doesn't exist. Additionally, I notice the command is missing the `--base_model` and `--sft_adapter` parameters that were present in the successful versions.

Looking at what a proper command should include, I can see it needs to specify the base model path, the SFT adapter location, the training data, output directory, learning rate, number of epochs, batch size settings, LoRA configuration, evaluation data, and other hyperparameters like the wandb run name and seed. The command is missing several critical arguments like `--base_model`, `--sft_adapter`, and key hyperparameters, but the immediate failure is caused by running from the wrong working directory. I should also verify whether the new flags `--weighted_sampling` and `--bf16` are intentional additions for this CED run, though the core issue remains the incorrect cwd and incomplete argument list. Let me check the GPU status to understand the broader context.

  "text": "## 审查报告 — rsft_ced_s43_train 失败\n\n**结论**: CRITICAL\n\n### 发现\n\n1. **[severity: critical] 训练命令有两处根本性错误，34.8 秒即崩溃**：\n\n   **错误 A — 工作目录错误**：命令使用 `cd /root/autodl-tmp/freige`，但 `python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer` 需要 `freige` 包在 Python path 中。所有之前成功的 RSFT 训练均使用 `cd /root/autodl-tmp`。日志仅一行错误：`No module named freige.training.rsft_trainer`。\n\n   **错误 B — 缺少关键参数**：对比成功的 flat-NLI s43 训练命令，CED s43 命令缺少以下必需参数：\n   - `--base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`\n   - `--sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`\n   - `--learning_rate 2e-5`\n   - `--num_epochs 3`\n   - `--per_device_batch_size 1`\n   - `--gradient_accumulation_steps 16`\n   - `--lora_rank 64`\n   - `--lora_alpha 128`\n\n   即使修复工作目录，缺少 `--base_model` 和 `--sft_adapter` 也会导致再次失败。\n\n2. **[severity: warning] cuda:1 持续空转** — 训练立即崩溃后 cuda:1 仍为 0% util / 0 MiB。CED s43 是 paired t-test 阻塞项，每分钟空转都在浪费预算。\n\n### 建议\n\n修复后重提交命令参考（基于 plan_001_flat_nli_seed43 成功模板，仅改数据路径和 seed）：\n```\ncd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer \\\n  --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B \\\n  --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output \\\n  --rsft_data_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl \\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s43 \\\n  --learning_rate 2e-5 --num_epochs 3 \\\n  --per_device_batch_size 1 --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 \\\n  --lora_rank 64 --lora_alpha 128 \\\n  --eval_data_dir /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred \\\n  --wandb_run_name rsft_ced_s43 --seed 43\n```\n注：原命令中的 `--weighted_sampling` 和 `--bf16` 需确认是否为 CED RSFT 特有配置（之前 CED s42/s44 训练是否使用过这些 flag），否则应去掉以保持与已有 CED seed 的超参一致性。",

Key findings:

1. **Data path inconsistency confirmed**: 
   - Original CED RSFT data: `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` = 2718 samples
   - `rsft_ced_s45_train_r2` plans to use: `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_ced_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` = 7942 samples
   
   This is a 2.9x difference in data size. For a paired t-test where you need controlled comparisons, using different training data for different seeds would invalidate the comparison. 

CED s42 and s44 presumably used the 2718-sample dataset, so s45 with nearly 3x more data won't be comparable. Meanwhile, cuda:1 is sitting completely idle while CED s43 failed and hasn't been retried yet. I also notice decisions.yaml jumped from _next_id: 72 to 74, meaning two new decisions got added. The "ign_f1_sync" worker is still a bit opaque to me—the name suggests it synced Ign-F1 values, but I'm not seeing the full picture of what changed beyond some updates to rsft_ced_s43_train notes.

Looking at the data path inconsistency more carefully, the original CED RSFT runs used `rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` with 2718 samples (CED-scored, top-k=1 filtered), while the new path points to `rsft_filtered_topk3_ced_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` with 7942 samples (top-k=3 data that's also CED-filtered). These are fundamentally different datasets, so I need to check what D072 and D073 actually are to understand if this was intentional.

Wait, I'm seeing that s43 also used the original 2718-sample dataset, so the inconsistency is isolated to s45_r2 which switched to the 7942-sample version. That's a critical methodological problem — changing the training data between seeds defeats the purpose of seed comparison, which should only vary random initialization.

    hierarchy"。plan_015 仍有价值（量化 QLoRA 放大效应）但不再是叙事关键。另需排查 Ign-F1=F1 的 eval pipeline
    bug。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2

  - exp_id: plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval

  - exp_id: mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1
    role: grpo_qlora
    result: catastrophic, rel_f1<0.02
  depends_on:

- id: D070
  date: 2026-05-15 23:50 UTC
  summary: Claim 4 重新定位：EDCR 从跨方法诊断指标降级为 per-relation-type/per-document 细粒度诊断工具
  rationale: plan_003 EDCR diagnostic table 显示跨方法 EDCR 与 rel_f1 近乎完全反相关（Spearman=-0.965），与
    evi_f1 相关性为零（0.002）。这意味着在方法级比较中 EDCR 不提供超出 rel_f1 的独立信息，原始 Claim 4"EDCR 捕捉 Evi-F1
    盲区"在方法级不成立。但 per-relation-type 分析显示有意义的方差（date_of_birth EDCR≈0.1 vs head_of_government
    EDCR≈0.95），说明 EDCR 在细粒度诊断上仍有价值。重新定位为"per-relation/per-document 证据质量诊断工具"，避免审稿人用一个相关性计算即击穿
    Claim 4。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic
    role: diagnostic_table
    result: EDCR vs rel_f1 Spearman=-0.965, vs evi_f1 Spearman=0.002
  depends_on: []
- id: D071
  date: 2026-05-15 23:50 UTC
  summary: EDCR 定义存在 by-construction 混淆：当前 EDCR ≈ f(1/rel_f1)，不独立度量 evidence 质量。暂停
    Claim 4，实现 TP-only EDCR 后重新评估
  rationale: plan_003 诊断表显示 EDCR vs rel_f1 Spearman r=-0.9648, EDCR vs evi_f1 r=0.0022。机理：EDCR
    = n_distractor/n_total 在全部预测上计算，rel_f1 低的模型预测大量错误关系→evidence 自动成为 distractor→推高
    EDCR。这是定义层面的混淆，非数据问题。修复方案：EDCR 改为仅在 TP triples 上计算（"正确关系中 evidence 的忠实度"），若 TP-only
    EDCR 仍与 rel_f1 强相关则 Claim 4 需大幅削弱。plan_013（人工标注验证）暂停，等 TP-only EDCR 定义确认后再决定。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic
    role: diagnostic
    result: EDCR vs rel_f1 r=-0.965, EDCR vs evi_f1 r=0.002
  depends_on: []
- id: D072
  date: 2026-05-16 00:00 UTC
  summary: 'Claim 4 升级为双层论述: 方法级 EDCR (decoupling 量化) + per-relation EDCR (独立诊断工具)'
  rationale: Per-relation EDCR 分析 (93 relation types) 显示 EDCR vs evi_f1 ρ=-0.064 (p=0.583)，完全独立。与方法级
    ρ=-0.965 形成互补。方法级 EDCR 本质是 rel_f1 的变换，诊断价值有限；但 per-relation 粒度上 EDCR 捕捉到 evi_f1
    看不到的信息（如 head_of_government EDCR=0.956 但 evi_f1=0.875，证据看似好但 relation 抽取极差）。同时发现
    evi_f1 vs rel_f1 在 per-relation 层面也近乎零相关 (ρ=-0.013)，说明 evidence quality 和 relation
    extraction 在不同 relation type 间完全解耦——这是一个独立的发现，强化整篇论文的 decoupling narrative。
  related_experiments:
  - exp_id: plan_003_edcr_diagnostic
    role: 方法级 EDCR 分析

  - exp_id: D070

    仅用于方法级快速对比；per-relation 表格须 n_pred≥10 过滤'
  rationale: 'Reviewer 指出两个问题: (1) EDCR formula 1-rel_f1/evi_f1 在 rel_f1>evi_f1 时为负(manufacturer=-0.1879)，名为
    rate 却不在[0,1]，审稿人会质疑; (2) per-relation top/bottom 表被退化 case 主导(n_pred=0→EDCR=0,
    TP=0→EDCR=1)，有 cherry-picking 嫌疑。解决方案: 论文正文 EDCR 定义为 distractor count ratio (cited
    evidence 中非支撑性证据的比例，自然∈[0,1])；formula proxy 仅在方法级无逐预测 distractor 计数时使用，论文中注明是近似。per-relation
    展示表格加 n_pred≥10 过滤。'

**D072**: Claim 4 upgraded to dual-layer: method-level EDCR (decoupling quantification) + per-relation EDCR (independent diagnostic tool). The per-relation analysis shows EDCR vs evi_f1 ρ=-0.064 (p=0.583) — completely independent at per-relation granularity. This is actually a reasonable response to the EDCR concerns I raised.

**D073**: EDCR paper definition normalized: use distractor count ratio (∈[0,1]) in text, formula proxy 1-rel_f1/evi_f1 only for method-level quick comparison. 

Per-relation tables need to filter for n_pred≥10 to avoid negative values and prevent cherry-picking. Now I'm trying to understand what the "ign_f1_sync" worker actually accomplished — based on D069, it seems to have investigated the Ign-F1 = F1 pipeline bug and possibly updated registry entries.

Looking at the registry, I notice `reeval_flat_nli_s43` shows `ign_f1: 0.4523` matching `rel_f1: 0.4523`, which makes sense for DocRED since train and dev use different documents with no overlapping triples to ignore. The worker likely verified this behavior was correct rather than a bug, then synced that understanding across the system.

The critical issue I'm spotting is a data path mismatch for `rsft_ced_s45_train_r2` — it's pointing to a filtered topk3 dataset with 7942 samples, while the original CED RSFT runs used a different path with only 2718 samples. This breaks the consistency needed for valid paired t-tests across seeds. I should verify what data the earlier CED runs actually used before concluding this is definitely wrong.

Looking at the available evidence, it seems `rsft_ced_s43_train` used the 2718-sample path, while there are separate topk3 and flat-NLI variants with their own sample counts, and the topk3_ced_filtered version appears to be a CED-filtered subset of the larger topk3 dataset.

Now I'm realizing the critical issue: if `rsft_ced_s45_train_r2` is actually trained on the topk3 CED-filtered data (7942 samples) rather than the original 2718-sample CED data, then it's not a valid seed variant for the paired t-test comparison. The paired comparison requires all four seeds (s42/s43/s44/s45) to use the same 2718-sample dataset with only the random seed varying.

I should flag this as a critical data path mismatch in my report, and also verify whether the "ign_f1_sync" worker's conclusion that Ign-F1=F1 is expected behavior aligns with my earlier analysis.
